  - «| `sdd id next` | Reserves the next id (`0001`–`9999`) in `ids.mode: sequence`: `id next --project-root "<root>" --reserve [--count N]`. In 3.0.0 it is a row of Now or Next if it will be done, with its id, title, one-liner, brief and size; or of Later, with its id, if it is not decided, or in the template section the user names. Raising a Later row to Next keeps its id and writes its brief and size. A pending patch is a row of Now with `**Carril:** patch` in its brief. In 2.x it is a row of «Próximo» wi»
- S400 · 2 frases · otra sección · skills/sdd-roadmap/SKILL.md:100 · 686ba9fb ↔ skills/sdd-roadmap/SKILL.md:109 · 14f69886
  - «1. **Inventory** — Later (2.x: Backlog), the technical debt (the «Lista» of `debt.md`; 2.x: «Deuda técnica»), and the «Propuestas» and action items of the newest `.docs/sdd/releases/v*/retro.md`, if any. Technical debt enters only if it is a verifiable prerequisite of an item in scope or by the user's explicit decision, not in bulk.»
  - «Triage decides the state of each item of the technical debt (3.0.0: `debt.md`; 2.x: «Deuda técnica» of `roadmap.md`). Each item of «Por triar» goes to exactly one of three states.   - «**Discuss with the user before attempting more fixes**: the thread stops as at a deviation from the approved approach ([control-profiles.md](../sdd-propose/references/control-profiles.md)); an implementer returns `BLOCKED` with the three attempts and what each one showed»
  - «**Exceptions** — the kit's lanes skip the cycle: a **config** change runs its gate instead (`sdd-propose`), and a **spike** task carries «Evidencia» instead of RED tests.   - «- **Cognitive load** is the cost on the human: which documents exist and when to reach for each. The human is the index. Not a cost to minimise: it is the price of human agency; spend it where human judgement matters, remove it where it does not.»
  - «When user-invoked skills multiply past what you can remember, that piled-up cognitive load is cured by a **router skill**: one user-invoked skill that names the others and when to reach for each, so the human has one skill to remember instead of many. It can only hint, never fire them: user-invoked skills have no description, so nothing but the human can reach them.»

  - «- **Native**: you implement every task in this session, with the ledger: no implementer subagent per task, no reviewer per task, and one fresh-context review of the whole branch at the end.   - «- **SDD** (`subagent`): a fresh implementer per task and a fresh reviewer after each, then the whole-branch review. Most thorough; a fresh context per task and per review.»
